Let's design fast delta update binary search in detail. For given n, m=n//2. h binary. c vector length 2n-1 indexed by shift s= -(n-1) ... n-1? We can index pos and neg separately? Need objective max over all. But updates for swap as above. Use full c array for easy max. For initial c compute with np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'). Note np.correlate output order: c[k] = ∑ a[i] b[i + k - (n-1)]? For k=0 shift=1-n, k=n-1 shift=0, k=2n-2 shift=n-1. Our shift order ascending.
When updating c with deltas computed by formulas, we must match this convention.
Let's set shifts array s = np.arange(-(n-1), n). Index idx = s + n-1.
Formula for change if h_i from old to new, h_j old to new. We can compute exact delta by evaluating affected terms. But vectorized formulas using ranges:
For h_i change Δ_i (new-old), h_j Δ_j. c_s = ∑ p_t (1-p_{t+s}). Change due to p_t. Derivative exact for binary? Since p_t changes ±1, and p_{t+s} may also change. The linear delta using old values plus cross term if both endpoints change. General exact: For each term (t, t+s), old=f(p_t,p_u), new=f(p_t+Δ_t,p_u+Δ_u). For binary, if no both endpoints same index? Terms where two changed endpoints: if {t,t+s} = {i,j}. Then old/new. We can handle by summing linear deltas then correcting double-count for pairs where both endpoints change? Let's derive exact formula using changes.
For a term with both endpoints not changing: no change. If one endpoint changes:
- source changes Δ: old a, new a+Δ; target b unchanged. term diff = (a+Δ)(1-b)-a(1-b) = Δ(1-b).
- target changes Δ: source a unchanged; diff = a(1-b-Δ)-a(1-b)= -a Δ.
If both endpoints change (source i Δ_i, target j Δ_j or vice versa): term old p_t(1-p_u), new (p_t+Δ_t)(1-(p_u+Δ_u)) = old + Δ_t(1-p_u) - p_t Δ_u - Δ_t Δ_u. Linear formula summing both endpoint contributions gives Δ_t(1-p_u_old) - p_t_old Δ_u + if both? If we sum linear source for i and target for j: Δ_i(1-p_j_old) - p_i_old Δ_j. Need add -Δ_i Δ_j. If source i target j, Δ_i=-1, Δ_j=+1, -Δ_i Δ_j = -(-1*+1)=+1. Let's test old (1,0) term=1; new (0,1) term=0; delta=-1. Linear: -1*(1-0) - 1*1 = -2; correction +1 => -1. Good. If old (0,1): term=0; new (1,0): term=1 delta=1. Linear: -1? source i 0->? wait i=1? Let's not. Works.
So exact delta = sum of endpoint linear contributions over valid terms - correction for each shift where i and j are exactly source/target pair (distance s=±(j-i)).
Our previous formulas for separate index changes without correction would be wrong if swap creates/deletes adjacent pair terms. Need include correction.
But we can avoid correction by simpler exact recompute of affected terms? Could compute delta by setting old and new and using formulas per endpoint then adjust if |i-j| shift exists twice? For swap i 1->0, j 0->1. There are potentially two affected terms at shift s=j-i (source i target j) and s=i-j (source j target i). Linear contributions from both endpoints in those terms double? Correction only for those shifts.

Smooth projected gradient line search:
Use beta anneal. Define smooth_value(c): f=c*dx; m=f.max(); return (m + log(sum(exp(beta*(f-m))))/beta). For beta large, use logsumexp stable. Use np.
During iteration, we need c for p. compute_c_fft. Also for candidate p_trial, compute c_trial and smooth. But line search with multiple c_trial costly. Since we have gradient, we can use fixed step with diminishing. Maybe use automatic backtracking only if smooth worsens significantly.
Pseudo:
p = feasible
best = inf
lr = 0.1
for beta in [4,8,16,32,64,128]:
  for it in range(100):
    c=compute_c(p); Cc=c.max()*dx; update best
    w=softmax(beta*c*dx)
    g=grad_c(c,w)*dx
    # normalize? If norm zero break.
    norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if norm<1e-10: break
    step_lr = lr / max(1.0, np.max(np.abs(g)))? Since g in C units, max maybe ~1. Use lr=0.1.
    y = p - step_lr*g
    q = project(y,S)
    c2=compute_c(q); C2=c2.max()*dx; s2=smooth(c2,beta)
    if C2 < Cc - 1e-12 or s2 < smooth_current - 1e-12:
       p=q; Cc=C2; if C2<best...
       lr = min(1.0, lr*1.1)
    else:
       lr *= 0.5
       if lr < 1e-8: break
    # Occasionally compute real best
  # after beta, maybe project exactly.

Annealing and restarts:
def binary_local_search(h, n, rng, time_limit, target_c5=None, max_trials=10**9):
  h = h.copy(); ensure binary half.
  inv = 1-h; h_rev=h[::-1]
  c = correlate_full(h)
  current_max = float(c.max())
  best_h = h.copy(); best_max = current_max; best_c5 = current_max*dx
  initialize lists.
  start=time
  trial=0
  T = 4.0 # count units
  last_recompute=0
  while time remaining and trial<max_trials:
    if trial % 2000 == 0:
       # maybe recompute c to correct drift
       c = correlate_full(h); current_max=c.max()
       # update best? If c max differs? best_h separate
    # Choose move
    if rng.rand() < 0.15 and current_max > best_max? target worst: generate bad_i; if fail random.
    else: random i from ones_list, j zeros_list.
    old_max = current_max
    apply_forward(c,h,inv,h_rev,i,j,n)  # uses old h/inv
    new_max = float(c.max())
    accept = False
    tol=1e-9
    if new_max < old_max - tol:
       accept=True
    elif new_max <= old_max + tol:
       accept = rng.rand() < 0.35
    else:
       # simulated acceptance for worse by small
       # T = max(0.5, initial_T * (1 - trial/max_iters)^2); but use time
       if T > 0.51:
          delta = new_max - old_max
          if delta <= T and np.exp(-delta/T) > rng.rand(): accept=True
    if accept:
       # update h/inv/lists
       h[i]=0; h[j]=1; inv[i]=1; inv[j]=0
       remove/add lists
       current_max = new_max
       if new_max < best_max - tol:
          best_max = new_max; best_h = h.copy(); best_c5=best_max*dx
       # if current drift far from best? if current_max > best_max + 2*T maybe reset? But keep.
       trial_accepted++
    else:
       apply_backward(...)
    trial++
    # T decay based on trial or elapsed
    T = max(0.0, 4.0 * (1.0 - elapsed/time_limit)**2) maybe. If time_limit large, T decays slowly. Use T = max(0.5, 4.0 * (1 - elapsed/time_limit))**2? If 0, no worse.
  return best_h,best_c5,best_max

   if cnt>0: h[interior] += diff/cnt; h=np.clip(h,0,1); s2=h.sum(); if not close, adjust largest interior.
   else: if diff>0 set some zeros to 1 else ones to 0 to make count target (floor?). Since target integer if n even. Need choose n even. We'll use even n. diff integer-ish. If h binary but sum wrong: if diff>0, set first int(diff) zeros to 1; if diff<0 set -int(diff) ones to 0. Then exact.

def optimize_continuous(p, n, time_limit, rng, seed, start_beta=4, end_beta=100, max_beta_steps=6):
 p=make_feasible(p,n); best=compute_c5_direct(p)
 dx=2.0/n; S=n/2.
 betas = np.geomspace(start_beta, end_beta, num=6) if start_beta>0.
 current_lr=0.1
 last_recheck time.
 for beta in betas:
   for it in range(... until time):
     c=compute_c_fft(p,n)
     Cc=c.max()*dx
     if Cc < best: best=Cc; best_p=p.copy()
     f=c*dx
     # stable logsumexp
     m=f.max(); ex=np.exp(beta*(f-m)); total=ex.sum(); w=ex/total
     G=compute_gradient_c(c,w,n)
     grad=dx*G
     # If gradient tiny break
     gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
     if gnorm < 1e-12: break
     # normalize lr? Perhaps step = lr / (1+0.1*gnorm?)
     step = current_lr / (1.0 + 0.05*gnorm) ? Since gnorm maybe 1; step 0.095.
     y = p - step * grad
     q = project_to_simplex(y,S)
     c2=compute_c_fft(q,n); C2=c2.max()*dx; s2=smooth_value(c2,beta,dx)
     # current smooth s? compute for line search
     s = (m + np.log(total)/beta) # since m + log(total)/beta? Actually f max m, sum exp = total, smooth = m + log(total)/beta if f in C units and beta applied. Yes.
     if C2 <= Cc - 1e-14 or s2 < s - 1e-14:
        p=q; Cc=C2; current_lr = min(1.0, current_lr*1.05)
     else:
        current_lr *= 0.5
        if current_lr < 1e-9: break
   # after beta, maybe direct recompute best.
 return best_p,best

But project_to_simplex uses y; if y+λ range. Need ensure q sum exact S. Let's implement efficient:
def project_to_simplex(y, S):
    # y length n. Find lam by bisection.
    lo = min(-y.max() - 1.0, 1.0 - y.min() - 1.0)? Wait y+lam clipped. To get sum 0, need lam ≤ -max(y). To get sum n, lam ≥ 1 - min(y). So lower = min(-y.max(), 1-y.min()) - 1 could be too low. Use lo = -y.max() - 1.0; hi = 1.0 - y.min() + 1.0.
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(y+mid,0,1))
       if s < S: lo=mid else hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    return np.clip(y+lam,0,1)
Will sum near S. Then exact fix:
 q= np.clip(y+lam,0,1); diff=S-q.sum(); if abs(diff)>1e-12: interior=(q>1e-12)&(q<1-1e-12); if count: q[interior]+=diff/count; clip and final adjust one.
Return q.

def binary_anneal(h_init, n, rng, time_limit, target_c5=None):
   h = np.array(h_init, dtype=float).copy()
   # ensure binary half: use top_n_binary(h,n,rng) to quantize.
   h = top_n_binary(h, n, rng)
   c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
   current_max = float(c.max())
   best_h = h.copy(); best_max=current_max; best_c5=current_max*(2.0/n)
   # lists
   ones = np.nonzero(h>0.5)[0].tolist()? Need fast random pop.
   zeros = ... list
   pos_one = np.full(n, -1, dtype=int); pos_zero=...
   fill.
   inv=1-h; rev=h[::-1].copy()
   deadline=time.time()+time_limit
   start=time.time()
   T=4.0
   trial=0; last_rec=0
   n1=n-1; two_n1=2*n-1
   worst_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
   while time.time() < deadline:
      # time chunk maybe 10000
      for _ in range(5000):
         if time.time() >= deadline: break
         # choose move
         i_j? We need list lengths.
         target = (rng.random() < 0.2) and (worst_idx is not None)
         if target:
            s = worst_idx - n1
            # use np.where on h slices; but h list? h array.
            bad=None
            if s == 0:
               pass
            elif s > 0:
               # valid i 0..n-s-1
               bad = np.flatnonzero((h[:n-s] > 0.5) & (h[s:] < 0.5))
               if bad.size:
                  i = int(bad[rng.randint(bad.size)])
               else: i = ones_list[rng.randint(len(ones_list))]
            else:
               sa=-s
               bad = np.flatnonzero((h[sa:] > 0.5) & (h[:n-sa] < 0.5)) + sa
               if bad.size: i=int(...)
               else i=ones_list[...]
            # choose zero j; maybe if target zero is in bad? We can if bad pair target stored? Could find j = i+s if s>0 and h[j]==0; likely. Use it if valid and in zeros.
            if s>0:
               cand = i+s
               if 0 <= cand < n and h[cand] < 0.5 and cand != i: j=cand
               else: j=zeros_list[...]
            elif s<0:
               cand = i+s  # negative shift target i-abs(s)? For s=-sa, target i-sa = i+s.
               if valid and h[cand]<0.5: j=cand
               else: j=zeros_list[...]
            else: i=ones_list[...]; j=zeros_list[...]
         else:
            li=len(ones_list); if li==0 break; lz=len(zeros_list); if lz==0 break
            i=ones_list[rng.randint(li)]
            j=zeros_list[rng.randint(lz)]
         if i == j: continue
         old_max=current_max
         # apply forward
         start_i = n1 - i
         end_i = 2*n1 + 1 - i
         c[start_i:end_i] -= inv
         c[i:n+i] += rev
         start_j = n1 - j
         end_j = 2*n1 + 1 - j
         c[start_j:end_j] += inv
         c[j:n+j] -= rev
         idx1 = n1 + (j - i)
         idx2 = n1 + (i - j)
         c[idx1] += 1.0
         c[idx2] += 1.0
         new_max = float(c.max())
         accept=False
         if new_max < old_max - 1e-12:
             accept=True
         elif new_max <= old_max + 1e-12:
             accept = rng.random() < 0.4
         else:
             # anneal
             if T > 0.51:
                delta = new_max - old_max
                if delta <= T:
                   # use rng.random; exp
                   if rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / T): accept=True
         if accept:
            # update data
            h[i]=0.0; h[j]=1.0
            inv[i]=1.0; inv[j]=0.0
            ii = n1 - i; jj = n1 - j
            rev[ii]=0.0; rev[jj]=1.0
            # lists remove/add
            # remove i from ones
            pi=pos_one[i]
            last=ones_list[-1]
            ones_list[pi]=last
            pos_one[last]=pi
            ones_list.pop()
            pos_one[i]=-1
            # remove j from zeros
            p0=pos_zero[j]
            last0=zeros_list[-1]
            zeros_list[p0]=last0
            pos_zero[last0]=p0
            zeros_list.pop()
            pos_zero[j]=-1
            # add j to ones
            pos_one[j]=len(ones_list); ones_list.append(j)
            # add i to zeros
            pos_zero[i]=len(zeros_list); zeros_list.append(i)
            current_max = new_max
            if new_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                best_max = new_max
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c5 = best_max * (2.0/n)
                worst_idx = int(np.argmax(c))  # update maybe costly but improvement rare
            # maybe update worst if current_max high? not every trial.
         else:
            # backward
            c[start_i:end_i] += inv
            c[i:n+i] -= rev
            c[start_j:end_j] -= inv
            c[j:n+j] += rev
            c[idx1] -= 1.0
            c[idx2] -= 1.0
         trial += 1
         if (trial & 1023) == 0:
             # recompute c every 1024? 1024 recompute too many? n=4096 recompute 16M*1000=16B no. Use larger 50000.
         if trial % 20000 == 0:
             c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
             current_max=float(c.max())
             if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                 best_max=current_max; best_h=h.copy(); best_c5=best_max*(2.0/n)
             worst_idx=int(np.argmax(c))
             # reset T maybe
         # T decay
         elapsed=time.time()-start
         T = max(0.0, 4.0 * (1.0 - elapsed/time_limit))**2
         if T < 0.05: T=0.0
      # after chunk, maybe if current_max > best_max + 10 and time allow, reset to best_h
      if current_max > best_max + 8.0:
          # reset to best to avoid drift too far
          h = best_h.copy()
          c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
          current_max=float(c.max())
          inv=1-h; rev=h[::-1].copy(); rebuild lists (function rebuild_binary_sets)
          worst_idx=int(np.argmax(c))
   return best_h, best_c5

Rebuilding lists function:

def compute_c_fft(p, n):
    N = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()? Using next power of two faster than 2n if not power. For n powers, N=2n which is power. For other n, choose N=next power >=2n. But we'll use powers for best. Implement:
    N = 1
    while N < 2*n - 1: N <<=1
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conj(F), N)[:n].real
    prefix = np.cumsum(p); full prefix with 0.
    c = np.empty(2*n-1)
    c[n-1] = p.sum() - ac[0]
    # vectorize for s=1..n-1
    ss = np.arange(1,n)
    c[n-1+ss] = prefix[n-ss?]. Need prefix length n+1.
    pos_source = prefix[n-ss]  # sum first n-s
    c[n-1+ss] = pos_source - ac[ss]
    c[n-1-ss] = S - prefix[ss] - ac[ss]
    return c

compute_gradient_c(c,w,n):
    n1=n-1
    w_plus = np.zeros(n); w_plus[1:] = w[n1+1:]? Full index n1+1 to 2n-2 corresponds s=1..n-1. w[n1+1: n1+n] length n-1. So w_plus[1:] = w[n1+1:]
    w_minus = np.zeros(n); w_minus[1:] = w[:n1][::-1]? For index n1-s corresponds s. w[0] shift -(n-1), ... w[n1-1] shift -1. So s=1 index n1-1, s=n1 index0. w_minus[1:n] = w[n1-1::-1] (length n-1). Simpler:
       w_minus[1:] = w[:n1][::-1]
    w0=w[n1]
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p,n? Need N same as c.). For gradient transforms, use N next power >=2n-1.
    Fw_p = np.fft.rfft(w_plus,N); Fw_m=...
    L_plus = np.fft.irfft(Fw_p * np.conj(Fp), N)[:n].real
    R_plus = np.fft.irfft(Fw_p * Fp, N)[:n].real
    L_minus = ...
    R_minus = ...
    tail_plus = np.zeros(n)
    # tail[i] = sum w_plus[i+1:n]
    suff = np.cumsum(w_plus[::-1])[::-1]  # suff[i]=sum w_plus[i:n]
    tail_plus = suff - w_plus
    prefix_minus = np.cumsum(w_minus)  # cum[0]=w0=0; cum[i]=sum 0..i = sum1..i
    G = (tail_plus - L_plus) + (prefix_minus - R_minus) - R_plus - L_minus
    G += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0*p)
    return G

Coordinate delta function (single coordinate):
def apply_coord_update(c, p, a, delta, n):
    # assumes 0<=p[a]+delta<=1; updates c for all shifts (including 0) in place, does not update p.
    old = p[a]; n1=n-1
    # positive source: c[n1+1 : 2*n-1-a] += delta*(1 - p[a+1:n])
    end_pos_src = 2*n-1 - a
    if end_pos_src > n1+1:
        c[n1+1:end_pos_src] += delta * (1.0 - p[a+1:n])
    # positive target: c[n1+1:n1+a+1] += -delta * p[:a][::-1]
    if a > 0:
        c[n1+1:n1+a+1] += -delta * p[:a][::-1]
    # negative source:
    if a > 0:
        c[n1-a:n1] += delta * (1.0 - p[:a])
    # negative target:
    start_neg_tgt = a
    if n-1-a > 0:
        c[a:n1] += -delta * p[a+1:n][::-1]
    # c0:
    c[n1] += delta*(1.0 - 2.0*old) - delta*delta
This uses view slices and creates temporaries for (1-p[a+1:n]) etc. It updates c O(n). It does not modify p. Good.

pos target none
neg src none
neg tgt c[0:1] += -(-1)*p[1:2][::-1]=+1*0=0
c0 += -1*(1-2)-1? δ=-1, old1: δ*(1-2old)= -1*(-1)=1; -δ^2=-1 total0. c [0,0,0]
Update p0=0.
j=1 δ=+1:
p now [0,1? no p1 old0, update p1 later]
pos src none (a=1 end 3? 2n-1-a=3? n1+1=2, c[2:3] length1? Wait a=1, n=2: end=3-1=2? 2*n-1-a =4-1-1=2, start2 => none)
pos target c[2:3] += -1*p[:1][::-1]= -1*0=0
neg src c[0:1] +=1*(1-p[:1])=1*(1-0)=1
neg tgt none
c0 old p1=0 δ=1: 1*(1) -1=0. c [1,0,0]. Good.

Function:
def coord_delta_update(c, p, a, delta, n):
    n1 = n - 1
    old = p[a]
    # positive source
    start = n1 + 1
    end = 2*n - 1 - a
    if end > start:
        c[start:end] += delta * (1.0 - p[a+1:n])
    # positive target
    if a > 0:
        c[start:n1 + a + 1] += -delta * p[:a][::-1]
    # negative source
    if a > 0:
        c[n1 - a:n1] += delta * (1.0 - p[:a])
    # negative target
    end = n1
    start2 = a
    if end > start2:
        c[start2:end] += -delta * p[a+1:n][::-1]
    # c0
    c[n1] += delta * (1.0 - 2.0*old) - delta*delta
Let's check positive target slice length: start n1+1, end n1+a+1. For a=0 none. Values p[:a][::-1] length a. Good.

But note positive source and negative target overlap? For a not 0, slices different positions. Good.

Implement function coordinate_anneal(p_init,n,rng,time_limit):
- p make_feasible
- c = compute_corr_direct(p) (np.correlate)
- current_max = c.max()
- best...
- steps = np.array([0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5])
- T_count = 2.0 maybe.
Loop time:
  choose i,j random distinct.
  direction: if p[i] >= p[j]? We want move probability mass from high to low? Actually i and j roles arbitrary; we choose direction such that p_i increases? The move is p_i += δ, p_j -= δ. We can pick sign = 1 if p_i > p_j? Because transferring mass from high to low might reduce c0? But objective not monotonic. For exploration, sign random. If sign random, we can let i be any and j any; if sign=-1, then p_i decreases p_j increases. We can implement sign = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.
  compute max_allowed:
    if sign>0: maxd = min(1-p[i], p[j])
    else: maxd = min(p[i], 1-p[j])
  if maxd <= 1e-14: continue
  delta = sign*fraction...
  old_i = p[i]; old_j=p[j]
  apply_coord_update(c,p,i,delta,n)
  p[i] = old_i + delta
  apply_coord_update(c,p,j,-delta,n)
  p[j] = old_j - delta
  new_max = c.max()
  accept irule. If reject:
     # revert order: revert j then i? Current p_i new, p_j new.
     # Revert j delta = +delta (from new to old_j) with p_i still new; p[j]=old_j
     apply_coord_update(c,p,j,delta,n)
     p[j]=old_j
     apply_coord_update(c,p,i,-delta,n)
     p[i]=old_i
  Track best.
- Periodically recompute c = np.correlate(p,1-p,'full') every e.g. 20000 trials; current_max = max; if improved best.
- T decay based count units.
- Return best_p,best_c5.

Let's plan final pipeline with coordinate local search (call pair_local_search):
1. Determine parent if available. parent_h = make_feasible(parent, len) maybe. Evaluate direct. best = parent.
2. Define local search function pair_anneal(p_init, n, rng, time_limit, target_count? status?) returns p,best_c5.
3. For each n in [512,1024,2048,4096,8192] (depending time), generate initial p inits:

Now need make_feasible for continuous exact. Implement:
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    target = n/2.0
    diff = target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        interior = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = interior.count
        if cnt > 0:
            h[interior] += diff / cnt
            h = np.clip(h,0,1)
        # if still not exact due boundaries
        diff = target - np.sum(h)
        if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
            if diff > 0:
               idxs = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
            else: idxs=np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-12)
            k = min(len(idxs), int(abs(diff)+1))
            h[idxs[:k]] += 1.0 if diff>0 else -1.0
            h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final adjust one interior if possible
    diff = target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       interior = np.flatnonzero((h>1e-8)&(h<1-1e-8))
       if interior.size:
          k=interior[0]
          h[k] = np.clip(h[k]+diff,0,1)
    return h
For binary target exactly integer, if h all 0/1 but count wrong, above sets. For continuous, sum maybe exact within float.
We also need if n even always.

Top_n_binary:
def top_n_binary(scores,n,rng):
    target=n//2
    # add tiny noise if needed? scores could have ties; use rng.normal(0,1e-7)
    if scores.size != n: interpolate? assume.
    # For exact target:
    h = np.zeros(n)
    if target ==0: return h
    if target == n: h.fill(1); return h
    # use argpartition; if many ties, random noise
    s = scores + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-9, n)
    part = np.argpartition(s, target-1)
    top = part[:target]
    h[top]=1.0
    return h
This uses RNG values; okay.

Mseq generation code:
def mseq_initial(n, rng):
   # n power of 2?
   m = int(round(np.log2(n)))
   if (1<<m) != n: return None
   taps = _MSEQ_GET(m)
   L=(1<<m)-1
   seq=[1]*m
   while len(seq)<L:
       t=len(seq)-m
       val=0
       for j in taps:
           val ^= seq[t+j]
       seq.append(val)
   base = seq + [0]
   phase = int(rng.randint(0, n)) if n>1 else 0
   base = base[phase:] + base[:phase]
   h = np.array(base, dtype=float)
   # ensure sum target
   return make_feasible(h,n)? But make_feasible may fractional if count wrong. Better adjust binary count:
   target=n//2; ones=sum(h); if ones != target: if ones>target flip random ones to 0 else zeros to1. Use rng.
   return h

Let's write pair_anneal in detail with local variables. Because no closures, we can use helper functions. It will return (best_p, best_c5). It should not modify input.
Pseudo code:
def pair_anneal(p_init, n, rng, time_limit):
    p = make_feasible(np.array(p_init,dtype=float), n)
    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0-p, mode='full')
    current_max = float(c.max())
    best_p = p.copy(); best_max = current_max; dx=2.0/n; best_c5=best_max*dx
    n1=n-1; target=n/2.0
    start=time.time(); deadline=start+time_limit
    trial=0; no_improve=0
    T=5.0
    steps = (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # loop chunk to check deadline maybe inner 2000
       for _ in range(2000):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          # choose move
          target_move = rng.random() < 0.15
          if target_move:
             worst_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
             s = worst_idx - n1
             i = None; j = None; sign = -1
             if s == 0:
                pass
             elif s > 0:
                score = p[:n-s] * (1.0 - p[s:])
                smax = float(score.max()) if score.size else 0.0
                if smax > 1e-12:
                   # choose among best? argmax
                   idx = int(np.argmax(score))
                   i = idx; j = idx + s
                # else random
             else:
                r = -s
                score = p[r:] * (1.0 - p[:n-r])
                if score.size:
                   idxr = int(np.argmax(score))
                   i = idxr + r; j = idxr
            if i is None:
               i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n); sign = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
          else:
             i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n); sign = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
          if i == j: continue
          pi = p[i]; pj = p[j]
          if sign > 0:
             maxd = min(1.0 - pi, pj)
          else:
             maxd = min(pi, 1.0 - pj)
          if maxd <= 1e-15:
             # maybe flip sign if possible?
             continue
          # choose delta
          if target_move:
             delta = sign * max(1e-15, maxd * rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))
          else:
             if rng.random() < 0.3:
                delta = sign * maxd * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.0)
             else:
                step = steps[rng.randint(len(steps))]
                absolute = min(step, maxd)
                if absolute < 1e-15:
                   absolute = maxd * 0.25
                delta = sign * absolute * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.0)
          old_max = current_max
          apply +...
          new_max = float(c.max())
          accept = ...
          if accept:
             current_max = new_max
             if new_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                 best_max = new_max; best_p = p.copy(); best_c5=best_max*dx; no_improve=0
             else: no_improve += 1? For accept non-improve yes.
          else:
             revert
             no_improve += 1
          trial +=1
          if (trial & 0x7fff) == 0: # every ~32768
             # recompute maybe if drift
             if abs(float(np.sum(p)) - target) > 1e-10 or (trial % 100000 == 0):
                 p = make_feasible(p,n)
                 c = np.correlate(p, 1.0-p, mode='full')
                 current_max = float(c.max())
                 if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                     best_max=current_max; best_p=p.copy(); best_c5=best_max*dx; no_improve=0
             if current_max > best_max + 20.0:
                 p = best_p.copy(); c=np.correlate(p,1-p,'full'); current_max=float(c.max()); no_improve=0
          # update T
          elapsed = time.time() - start
          frac = 1.0 - elapsed/time_limit
          if frac < 0: frac=0
          T = max(0.05, 5.0 * frac * frac)
       # after inner, if no_improve > 200000 and current_max > best_max + 1: reset
       if no_improve > 200000 and current_max > best_max + 1.0:
           p = best_p.copy(); c=np.correlate(...); current_max=...; no_improve=0
    # final make_feasible and evaluate?
    p = make_feasible(p,n)
    c = np.correlate(p,1-p,'full'); current_max=float(c.max())
    if current_max < best_max:
       best_max=current_max; best_p=p.copy()
    # also best_p final ensure
    best_p = make_feasible(best_p,n)
    bc5 = compute_c5_direct(best_p) # exact
    return best_p, bc5

Thue-morse function:
def thue_morse_binary(n):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n): h[i] = 1.0 if bin(i).count('1')%2==0 else 0.0
   return make_feasible(h,n)

def get_initial(n, kind, parent, rng):
   if kind=='cont':
      if parent is not None and len>0: scores = repeat_or_interp(parent,n); return make_feasible(scores,n)
      else: return thue_morse_binary(n)
   if kind=='bin':
      if parent: scores=repeat_or_interp(parent,n)+noise; return top_n_binary(scores,n,rng)
      else: return random_binary(n,rng)
   if kind=='mseq':
      h=mseq_initial(n,rng); if h is not None: return h
      else: return thue_morse_binary(n)

repeat_or_interp:
def repeat_or_interp(parent,n):
   m=len(parent)
   if n % m == 0:
      return np.repeat(parent, n//m)
   else:
      x_old=np.linspace(0,2,m,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); return np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent)

Let's write pair_anneal carefully.
Pseudo final:
def pair_anneal(p_init, n, rng, time_limit):
    p = make_feasible(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=float), n)
    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
    current_max = float(c.max())
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_max = current_max
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_c5 = best_max * dx
    n1 = n - 1
    target = 0.5 * n
    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, time_limit)
    steps = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0)
    T = 4.0
    trial = 0
    no_improve = 0
    # local references
    corr = np.correlate
    rand = rng.random
    randint = rng.randint
    while time.time() < deadline:
        # outer chunk 2048
        for _ in range(2048):
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            target_move = rand() < 0.12
            if target_move:
                worst_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
                s = worst_idx - n1
                i = -1; j = -1
                sign = -1
                if s > 0:
                    len_s = n - s
                    if len_s > 0:
                        # score = p[0:len_s] * (1-p[s:s+len_s])
                        score = p[:len_s] * (1.0 - p[s:s+len_s])
                        idx = int(np.argmax(score))
                        if score[idx] > 1e-12:
                            i = idx
                            j = idx + s
                elif s < 0:
                    r = -s
                    len_s = n - r
                    if len_s > 0:
                        score = p[r:] * (1.0 - p[:len_s])
                        idx = int(np.argmax(score))
                        if score[idx] > 1e-12:
                            i = idx + r
                            j = idx
                if i < 0:
                    i = int(randint(n))
                    j = int(randint(n))
                    sign = 1.0 if rand() < 0.5 else -1.0
            else:
                i = int(randint(n))
                j = int(randint(n))
                sign = 1.0 if rand() < 0.5 else -1.0
            if i == j:
                continue
            pi = p[i]; pj = p[j]
            if sign > 0:
                maxd = min(1.0 - pi, pj)
            else:
                maxd = min(pi, 1.0 - pj)
            if maxd <= 1e-14:
                continue
            # margin
            if maxd > 1.0:
                maxd = 1.0
            # choose delta
            if target_move:
                delta = sign * maxd * (0.35 + 0.65 * rand())
            else:
                full = rand() < 0.22
                if full:
                    delta = sign * maxd * (0.7 + 0.3 * rand())
                else:
                    step = steps[int(randint(len(steps)))]
                    absolute = step if step < maxd else maxd
                    if absolute < 1e-14:
                        absolute = maxd * 0.25
                    delta = sign * absolute * (0.6 + 0.4 * rand())
            if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
                continue
            old_max = current_max
            pi_old = pi; pj_old = pj
            coord_delta_update(c, p, i, delta, n)
            p[i] = pi + delta
            # small clip/snap
            if p[i] < 0.0: p[i] = 0.0
            elif p[i] > 1.0: p[i] = 1.0
            coord_delta_update(c, p, j, -delta, n)
            p[j] = pj - delta
            if p[j] < 0.0: p[j] = 0.0
            elif p[j] > 1.0: p[j] = 1.0
            new_max = float(c.max())
            accept = False
            if new_max < old_max - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif new_max <= old_max + 1e-12:
                if rand() < 0.35: accept = True
            elif new_max < old_max + T:
                # delta positive
                d = new_max - old_max
                if rand() < math.exp(-d / T): accept = True
            if accept:
                current_max = new_max
                if new_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                    best_max = new_max
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_c5 = best_max * dx
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                # revert
                coord_delta_update(c, p, j, delta, n)
                p[j] = pj_old
                coord_delta_update(c, p, i, -delta, n)
                p[i] = pi_old
                no_improve += 1
            trial += 1
            if (trial & 32767) == 0:
                # periodic cleanup
                if abs(float(np.sum(p)) - target) > 1e-10:
                    p = make_feasible(p, n)
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                        best_max = current_max; best_p = p.copy(); best_c5 = best_max*dx; no_improve=0
                if (trial % 100000) == 0:
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                        best_max = current_max; best_p = p.copy(); best_c5 = best_max*dx; no_improve=0
                reset_thresh = max(3.0, 0.05 * max(1.0, best_max))
                if current_max > best_max + reset_thresh:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    no_improve = 0
        if no_improve > 200000 and current_max > best_max + 1.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
            current_max = float(c.max())
            no_improve = 0
        elapsed = time.time() - start
        frac = 1.0 - elapsed / max(1.0, time_limit)
        if frac < 0.0: frac = 0.0
        T = max(0.05, 4.0 * frac * frac)
    # final
    p = make_feasible(p, n)
    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode='full')
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm < best_max:
        best_max = cm; best_p = p; best_c5 = cm*dx
    best_p = make_feasible(best_p, n)
    bc5 = compute_c5_direct(best_p)
    if bc5 < best_c5: best_c5=bc5
    return best_p, best_c5

In revert, after clipping p[i]/p[j] to bounds, the actual delta may differ; c not corrected. Clipping only if tiny. But we set p[j]=pj_old exact after revert. Good.    if desired <0: desired=0.0

# Clamp delta_abs to ensure no exceed due rounding:
if sign >0:

def repeat_or_interp(parent,n,linear):
    m=len(parent)
    if linear or n % m !=0:
       if m==n: return parent.copy()
       x_old=np.linspace(0,2.0,m,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)

import numpy as np
import time
import math

_MSEQ_TAPS = {
 2:(0,1), 3:(0,1), 4:(0,1), 5:(0,2), 6:(0,1), 7:(0,1),
 8:(0,2,3,4), 9:(0,4), 10:(0,3), 11:(0,2), 12:(0,1,4,6)

make_feasible: Need if h is length n but n maybe float? int.
Implementation:
def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
       # If different, resize? For safety, interpolate to n? But callers pass correct. If not:
       if h.size == 0: h = np.full(n,0.5)
       else:
          x_old=np.linspace(0,2,h.size,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       interior = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
       cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
       if cnt > 0:
          h[interior] += diff / cnt
          h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
       diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
          if diff > 0:
             idx = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
             if idx.size:
                k = min(idx.size, int(math.floor(diff + 1e-12)))
                if k > 0:
                   h[idx[:k]] += 1.0
                   h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
          else:
             idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-12)
             if idx.size:
                k = min(idx.size, int(math.floor(-diff + 1e-12)))
                if k > 0:
                   h[idx[:k]] -= 1.0
                   h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # final fine tune
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8))
        if idx.size:
           k = int(idx[0])
           h[k] = float(np.clip(h[k] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h

If diff not integer for continuous, k floor maybe 0; interior adjustment likely handles. Good.

mseq: ensure n power of 2. If m in taps. Generate seq. If count mismatch adjust. For m=2 L=3 base length4. For n=4 maybe m=2. The taps for m=2 maybe sequence weight? good.
Code:
def mseq_initial(n, rng):
    m = int(round(math.log2(n)))
    if m < 2 or (1 << m) != n: return None
    if m not in _MSEQ_TAPS: return None
    taps = _MSEQ_TAPS[m]
    L = (1 << m) - 1
    seq = [1] * m
    while len(seq) < L:
        t = len(seq) - m
        val = 0
        for j in taps:
            val ^= seq[t + j]
        seq.append(val)
    base = seq + [0]
    if n > 1:
       phase = int(rng.randint(0, n))
       base = base[phase:] + base[:phase]
    h = np.array(base, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    ones = int(np.count_nonzero(h > 0.5))
    if ones != target:
       if ones > target:
          idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
          k = ones - target
          flip = rng.choice(idx, size=k, replace=False)
          h[flip] = 0.0
       else:
          idx = np.flatnonzero(h <= 0.5)
          k = target - ones
          flip = rng.choice(idx, size=k, replace=False)
          h[flip] = 1.0
    return h
If rng.choice idx empty? If ones target no. If idx size < k? Shouldn't. But if mseq weight far? Use if idx.size>=k else adjust all. safe:
if k>0 and idx.size>0: flip=...; h[flip]=...
If not, return random? But primitive should. We'll guard.

repeat_or_interp:
def repeat_or_interp(parent,n,linear):
    m=parent.size
    if m == n: return parent.copy()
    if (not linear) and (n % m == 0):
        return np.repeat(parent, n // m)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent)

top_n_binary:
def top_n_binary(scores,n,rng):
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    target=n//2
    if target <=0: return h
    if target >= n: h.fill(1); return h
    s = np.asarray(scores,float)
    if s.size != n:
       s = repeat_or_interp(s,n,True)
    s = s + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-9, n)
    part = np.argpartition(s, target - 1)
    top = part[:target]
    h[top]=1.0
    return h

compute_c5_direct:
def compute_c5_direct(h):
   n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
   corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
   return float(np.max(corr)) * dx

Now, run function:
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
   np.random.seed(seed)
   rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
   start_time=time.time()
   deadline = start_time + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
   parent=None
   try:
       arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if arr.size > 0:
          parent = make_feasible(arr, arr.size)
   except Exception:
       parent = None
   if parent is None:
       parent = thue_morse_binary(256)
   best_h = parent.copy()
   best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
   best_n = len(best_h)

   stages = [...]
   # Ensure if budget small, maybe only first stage.
   for n, kind, frac in stages:
      remaining = deadline - time.time()
      if remaining < 15: break
      proposed = int(max(5.0, frac * (max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0))))
      limit = min(proposed, int(remaining - 5))
      if limit < 5: break
      if kind == 'cont':
          if parent is not None:
             init = repeat_or_interp(parent, n, False)
             init = make_feasible(init,n)
          else:
             init = thue_morse_binary(n)
      elif kind == 'bin':
          if parent is not None:
             scores = repeat_or_interp(parent, n, True)
             scores = scores + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-8, n)
             init = top_n_binary(scores, n, rng)
          else:
             init = random_binary(n, rng)
      elif kind == 'mseq':
          init = mseq_initial(n, rng)
          if init is None:
             init = thue_morse_binary(n)
      else:
          init = random_binary(n,rng)
      res_h, res_c = pair_anneal(init, n, rng, limit)
      # final eval again maybe
      if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
          res_h = make_feasible(res_h, n)
          res_c = compute_c5_direct(res_h)
          if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
              best_c5 = res_c; best_h = res_h; best_n = n
   # If time remains, run one more on best_n cont? maybe
   remaining = deadline - time.time()
   if remaining > 60:
       # try continuous polish of best at same n
       limit = int(remaining - 10)
       res_h, res_c = pair_anneal(best_h, best_n, rng, limit)
       if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-15:
           res_h=make_feasible(res_h,best_n); res_c=compute_c5_direct(res_h);
           if res_c < best_c5: best_c5=res_c; best_h=res_h
   # final
   best_h = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
   best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
   return (best_h, best_c5, best_n)

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_MSEQ_TAPS = {
    2: (0, 1),
    3: (0, 1),
    4: (0, 1),
    5: (0, 2),
    6: (0, 1),
    7: (0, 1),
    8: (0, 2, 3, 4),
    9: (0, 4),
    10: (0, 3),
    11: (0, 2),
    12: (0, 1, 4, 6),
}

_PAIR_STEPS = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0)


def compute_c5_direct(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr)) * dx


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
            h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    target = 0.5 * n
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        interior = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[interior] += diff / cnt
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
            else:
                idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-12)
            if idx.size:
                k = min(idx.size, int(math.floor(abs(diff) + 1e-12)))
                if k > 0:
                    h[idx[:k]] += 1.0 if diff > 0 else -1.0
                    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8))
        if idx.size:
            k = int(idx[0])
            h[k] = float(np.clip(h[k] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h


def thue_morse_binary(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        h[i] = 1.0 if (bin(i).count("1") % 2 == 0) else 0.0
    return make_feasible(h, n)


def random_binary(n, rng):
    h = (rng.rand(n) > 0.5).astype(float)
    target = n // 2
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h <= 0.5)
    if len(ones) > target:
        k = len(ones) - target
        if k and len(ones):
            flip = rng.choice(ones, size=k, replace=False)
            h[flip] = 0.0
    elif len(ones) < target:
        k = target - len(ones)
        if k and len(zeros):
            flip = rng.choice(zeros, size=k, replace=False)
            h[flip] = 1.0
    return h


def repeat_or_interp(parent, n, linear):
    m = int(parent.size)
    if m == n:
        return parent.copy()
    if (not linear) and (n % m == 0):
        return np.repeat(parent, n // m)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, parent)


def top_n_binary(scores, n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return h
    if target >= n:
        h.fill(1.0)
        return h
    s = np.asarray(scores, dtype=float)
    if s.size != n:
        s = repeat_or_interp(s, n, True)
    s = s + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-9, n)
    part = np.argpartition(s, target - 1)
    top = part[:target]
    h[top] = 1.0
    return h


def mseq_initial(n, rng):
    m = int(round(math.log2(n)))
    if m < 2 or (1 << m) != n:
        return None
    if m not in _MSEQ_TAPS:
        return None

    taps = _MSEQ_TAPS[m]
    L = (1 << m) - 1
    seq = [1] * m
    while len(seq) < L:
        t = len(seq) - m
        val = 0
        for j in taps:
            val ^= seq[t + j]
        seq.append(val)

    base = seq + [0]
    if n > 1:
        phase = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        base = base[phase:] + base[:phase]

    h = np.array(base, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    ones = int(np.count_nonzero(h > 0.5))
    if ones != target:
        if ones > target:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
            k = ones - target
            if idx.size and k > 0:
                flip = rng.choice(idx, size=min(k, idx.size), replace=False)
                h[flip] = 0.0
        else:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(h <= 0.5)
            k = target - ones
            if idx.size and k > 0:
                flip = rng.choice(idx, size=min(k, idx.size), replace=False)
                h[flip] = 1.0
    return h


def coord_delta_update(c, p, a, delta, n):
    n1 = n - 1
    old = p[a]

    start = n1 + 1
    end = 2 * n - 1 - a
    if end > start:
        c[start:end] += delta * (1.0 - p[a + 1:n])

    if a > 0:
        c[start:n1 + a + 1] += -delta * p[:a][::-1]

    if a > 0:
        c[n1 - a:n1] += delta * (1.0 - p[:a])

    if n - 1 - a > 0:
        c[a:n1] += -delta * p[a + 1:n][::-1]

    c[n1] += delta * (1.0 - 2.0 * old) - delta * delta


def pair_anneal(p_init, n, rng, time_limit):
    p = make_feasible(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=float), n)
    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
    current_max = float(c.max())

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_max = current_max
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_c5 = best_max * dx

    n1 = n - 1
    target = 0.5 * n
    start_t = time.time()
    deadline = start_t + max(1.0, float(time_limit))

    t0 = max(1.0, 0.006 * n)
    tmin = max(0.25, 0.0003 * n)
    T = t0

    trial = 0
    no_improve = 0
    rand = rng.random
    randint = rng.randint

    while time.time() < deadline:
        for _ in range(2048):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break

            target_move = (rand() < 0.12)
            if target_move:
                worst_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
                s = worst_idx - n1
                i = -1
                j = -1
                sign = -1.0

                if s > 0:
                    L = n - s
                    if L > 0:
                        score = p[:L] * (1.0 - p[s:s + L])
                        idx = int(np.argmax(score))
                        if score[idx] > 1e-12:
                            i = idx
                            j = idx + s
                elif s < 0:
                    r = -s
                    L = n - r
                    if L > 0:
                        score = p[r:] * (1.0 - p[:L])
                        idx = int(np.argmax(score))
                        if score[idx] > 1e-12:
                            i = idx + r
                            j = idx

                if i < 0:
                    i = int(randint(n))
                    j = int(randint(n))
                    sign = 1.0 if rand() < 0.5 else -1.0
            else:
                i = int(randint(n))
                j = int(randint(n))
                sign = 1.0 if rand() < 0.5 else -1.0

            if i == j:
                continue

            pi = p[i]
            pj = p[j]

            if sign > 0:
                maxd = min(1.0 - pi, pj)
            else:
                maxd = min(pi, 1.0 - pj)

            if maxd <= 1e-14:
                continue

            if target_move:
                delta_abs = maxd * (0.35 + 0.65 * rand())
            else:
                if rand() < 0.22:
                    delta_abs = maxd * (0.7 + 0.3 * rand())
                else:
                    step = _PAIR_STEPS[int(randint(len(_PAIR_STEPS)))]
                    absolute = step if step < maxd else maxd
                    if absolute < 1e-14:
                        absolute = maxd * 0.25
                    delta_abs = absolute * (0.6 + 0.4 * rand())

            if sign > 0:
                if pi + delta_abs > 1.0:
                    delta_abs = 1.0 - pi
                if pj - delta_abs < 0.0:
                    delta_abs = pj
            else:
                if pi - delta_abs < 0.0:
                    delta_abs = pi
                if pj + delta_abs > 1.0:
                    delta_abs = 1.0 - pj

            if delta_abs <= 1e-15:
                continue

            delta = sign * delta_abs
            old_max = current_max
            pi_old = pi
            pj_old = pj

            coord_delta_update(c, p, i, delta, n)
            p[i] = pi + delta
            coord_delta_update(c, p, j, -delta, n)
            p[j] = pj - delta

            new_max = float(c.max())
            accept = False

            if new_max < old_max - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif new_max <= old_max + 1e-12:
                if rand() < 0.35:
                    accept = True
            elif new_max < old_max + T:
                d = new_max - old_max
                if rand() < math.exp(-d / T):
                    accept = True

            if accept:
                current_max = new_max
                if new_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                    best_max = new_max
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_c5 = best_max * dx
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                coord_delta_update(c, p, j, delta, n)
                p[j] = pj_old
                coord_delta_update(c, p, i, -delta, n)
                p[i] = pi_old
                no_improve += 1

            trial += 1
            if (trial & 32767) == 0:
                if abs(float(np.sum(p)) - target) > 1e-10:
                    p = make_feasible(p, n)
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                        best_max = current_max
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_c5 = best_max * dx
                        no_improve = 0

                if (trial % 100000) == 0:
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    if current_max < best_max - 1e-12:
                        best_max = current_max
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_c5 = best_max * dx
                        no_improve = 0

                reset_thresh = max(3.0, 0.05 * max(1.0, best_max))
                if current_max > best_max + reset_thresh:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
                    current_max = float(c.max())
                    no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 200000 and current_max > best_max + 1.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
            current_max = float(c.max())
            no_improve = 0

        elapsed = time.time() - start_t
        frac = 1.0 - elapsed / max(1.0, float(time_limit))
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        T = max(tmin, t0 * frac * frac)

    p = make_feasible(p, n)
    c = np.correlate(p, 1.0 - p, mode="full")
    cm = float(c.max())
    if cm < best_max:
        best_max = cm
        best_p = p
        best_c5 = cm * dx

    best_p = make_feasible(best_p, n)
    bc5 = compute_c5_direct(best_p)
    if bc5 < best_c5:
        best_c5 = bc5
    return best_p, best_c5


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    np.random.seed(seed)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    start_time = time.time()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start_time + budget - 5.0

    parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if arr.size > 0:
            parent = make_feasible(arr, arr.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = thue_morse_binary(256)

    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
    best_n = len(best_h)

    stages = [
        (256, "cont", 0.02),
        (256, "bin", 0.01),
        (512, "cont", 0.03),
        (512, "bin", 0.02),
        (1024, "cont", 0.07),
        (1024, "bin", 0.05),
        (1024, "mseq", 0.02),
        (2048, "cont", 0.14),
        (2048, "bin", 0.09),
        (2048, "mseq", 0.03),
        (4096, "cont", 0.17),
        (4096, "bin", 0.08),
        (4096, "mseq", 0.03),
        (8192, "cont", 0.09),
        (8192, "bin", 0.04),
    ]

    for n, kind, frac in stages:
        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if remaining < 15.0:
            break

        proposed = int(max(5.0, frac * (budget - 30.0)))
        limit = min(proposed, int(remaining - 5.0))
        if limit < 5:
            break

        if kind == "cont":
            if parent is not None:
                init = repeat_or_interp(parent, n, False)
                init = make_feasible(init, n)
            else:
                init = thue_morse_binary(n)
        elif kind == "bin":
            if parent is not None:
                scores = repeat_or_interp(parent, n, True)
                scores = scores + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-8, n)
                init = top_n_binary(scores, n, rng)
            else:
                init = random_binary(n, rng)
        elif kind == "mseq":
            init = mseq_initial(n, rng)
            if init is None:
                init = thue_morse_binary(n)
        else:
            init = random_binary(n, rng)

        res_h, res_c = pair_anneal(init, n, rng, limit)
        if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            res_h = make_feasible(res_h, n)
            res_c = compute_c5_direct(res_h)
            if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = res_c
                best_h = res_h
                best_n = n

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 60.0:
        limit = int(remaining - 10.0)
        if limit >= 10:
            res_h, res_c = pair_anneal(best_h, best_n, rng, limit)
            if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                res_h = make_feasible(res_h, best_n)
                res_c = compute_c5_direct(res_h)
                if res_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = res_c
                    best_h = res_h

    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c5 = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
    return (best_h, best_c5, best_n)
```
